atha taittirīyopaniṣadbhāṣyavārtikasya prastāvaḥ |
iha khalu mārate varṣe sureśvarācāryapraṇītaṃ vārtikadvayaṃ tiṣṭhatīti suciraṃ
pravṛttirāsīt | etadvedāntasārabhūtagranthasamagrasaṭīkādarśa pustakālabhyatvā-
dutāssdarśapustakasaṃcayayatnaśaithilyāduta vedāntāmṛtāsvādānabhijñatvādvā
na kenāpyetāvatkālaparyantamekataramapi vārtikaṃ prakāśitam | etādṛśai-
vidvajjanānandakāraṇabhūtairgranthairaprasiddhayandhakāre nāvasthātavyamiti manasi
kṛtvā durlabhāprasiddha granthaprakāśanaikaheturānandāśramākhya mudraṇayantrālayā-
dhipatiridaṃ taittirīyakasārabhūtamadvaitānandapariplutaṃ śrīmacchaṃkarabhāṣyavive-
canarūpaṃ sureśvarācāryapraṇītaṃ vārtikaṃ prakāśatāmanaiṣīt |
atha ko'yaṃ sureśvarācāryaḥ kathaṃ sa vedāntaśāstrākṛtādaraḥ karmavā-
dyapi turīyāśramadīkṣāṃ gṛhītvā śrīmacchaṃkarācāryāṇāṃ śiṣyatvaṃ svīkṛ-
tavānitivicāraḥ prastūyate | atha kadācicchaṃkarācāryāḥ śrīmadvaipāyana-
niyogādvirodhavādigarvāṅkuronmūlanārthaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ pratasthire | tataḥ
sādhitakarmakāṇḍo bhaṭṭapāda iti prathitaḥ kumārilabhaṭṭa eva prathamaṃ jeya
iti manasi kṛtvā te tīrtharāja prayāgaṃ jagmuḥ | tatra triveṇītīre kṛta-
viśrāmā ācāryā gurūnmathanaprasaktamahādoṣamapākariṣṇurbhadvapādastuṣānalaṃ
praviṣṭa iti lokavārtāṃ śuśruvuḥ | tāṃ vārtāṃ śrutvā''cāryāḥ satvaraṃ gaccha -
ntastuṣarāśisaṃsthaṃ kumāramadrākṣuḥ | atha bhaṭṭapādo'pi dṛṣṭipathāvatīrṇā-
nparamagurūndṛṣṭvā parituṣṭacitto bhūtvā śiṣyagaṇaistānpūjayāmāsa | atha
gṛhītasaparyā bhagavantaḥ kumārilamidamūcuḥ | bho maha śuddhādvaitamārgaprada-
rśako'haṃ prasaṅgārthamāgato'smīti | tanniśamya bhaṭṭapādaḥ prāha | bho
gurvavajñādoṣanirasanārthaṃ tuṣāgnidehadahane'haṃ gṛhītadīkṣo'smi | vedokta-
saṃkalpitavatatyāgena yadyavakīrṇī bhaveyaṃ tarhi sadbhirvinindyo bhavitāsmi |
yadyadvaita mārgaprakāśanaikaheturbhagavāṃstarhi maṇḍanamiśranāmā viśvarūpa ityā-
khyayā bhuvi prasiddho mamāssvṛtto vijeyaḥ | sa ca yāyajūkapariṣatpra-
mukho mahāgṛhī karmavādī kāṇvaśākhīya udagdeśeṣu māhiṣmatyākhyāyāṃ
nagaryāṃ prativasati | tasmiñjite sarvaṃ jagajjitaṃ bhaviṣyatīti | eva-
muktvā sa bhaṭṭapādaḥ śrīmacchaṃkararūpamavekṣamāṇaḥ prāṇānatyajat |
atha tyaktamartyaloke bhaṭṭapāde maṇḍanamiśraṃ vijigīṣavaḥ śrīmadguravo
māhiṣmatī nagarīṃ yayuḥ | tatra gatvā maṇḍanamiśragṛhaṃ pihitadvāraṃ dṛṣṭvā

(2)
vyomamārgeṇaiva tatprāviśan | atha śrāddhavidhau pravṛtto maṇḍanamiśrastaṃ
yatīndraṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃvṛtadvāramapi magṛhaṃ prāpto'yaṃ ko bhavediti manasi kṛtvā
taṃ yatiśreṣṭhaṃ sāścaryaṃ pṛṣṭavānko bhavāniti | sa maskarīndro vivādabhikṣā-
micchanmavatsaṃnidhimāgato'smīti tamuvāca | atha maṇḍanamiśro'pi taṃ
tathā kartumanumene | tataḥ sa yatīndro maṇḍanamiśrasya vādotsukatāṃ vijñāya
tamuvāca mo yadyahaṃ parājitaḥ syāṃ tarhi saṃnyāsaṃ parityajya gṛhasthāśramī
bhaveyamiti | atha maṇḍanamiśro'pi tanniśamya bhavanāmamṛdyatimuvāca |
bho yadyahaṃ parājayabhāgbhaveyaṃ tarhi saṃnyāsamārga svī kuryāmiti | evaṃ

mādhavī śaṃkaravijaye-

śiṣyatvaṃ svīkṛtavān | atha dineṣu gacchatsu sureśvarācāryeṇā''tmaguruṃ
praṇamyedmavāci | bhagavanyanmama karaṇīyamasti tattvaṃ māmanuśādhīti |
atha tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śaṃkarācāryāstaṃ pratyavocanmama bhāṣyasya vārtikā -
khyo nibandhastvayā vidheya iti | guruvacanaṃ śrutvā sa sureśvarācāryo
bhavadīyaṃ śārīrabhāṣyaṃ satarka iṣṭuṃ yadi me śaktirnāsti tathā'pi bhavatā-
manugrahādeva yathāśakti nibandhanāyāhaṃ yata ityuktvā'stvevamityācārya-

bhāṣyaṃ karmaparaṃ bhaviṣyatīti | evaṃ bahu vidhāni nindāvacanāni nigadyā-
nena sureśvareṇa bhagavatāṃ bhāṣye nibandho naiva vidheya iti sarve'pi maga-
